Lucky signs: Rat, Dragon, Monkey · Careful: Horse
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.

## Luckiest animal today – Rat (ជូត) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color gold (មាស) · best time 15:00–17:00 · lucky numbers 3 · 39 · 232 · 3222 · 26323 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of an adorable golden rat with big sparkling eyes, tiny jade-green ear jewels and a proud little smile, the Rat of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness and new beginnings: a bright, clever and lively expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the rat, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video about the Rat, today's luckiest animal: 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Rat as ingredients. Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Rat, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Rat as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Rat's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Rat and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Rat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូតមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Rat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Thursday, 29 October 2026, is a Rat day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rat. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Rat are quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Rats are natural problem-solvers who notice opportunities others miss.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Rat? Your luck is strong today. Stay bright, clever and lively. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear something gold, or carry a small gold item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southwest. When you start something new, face the southwest, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 3, 39, and 232. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 3, 39, 232!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Rat's lucky days of the week are Monday and Tuesday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Rat can be restless or overly cautious with money and trust. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 3, 39, and 232.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. A new story every day: today the golden rat and Phleu the firefly must bring the bell's voice back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Phleu once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Rat picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Phleu and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

Adorable expressive face, warm magical glow, on a glowing pink lotus by a pond at night, a softly lit Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat and a full moon behind. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden rat bring the bell's voice back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the bell had lost its voice. When it was struck, there was only silence. Without its song, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden rat. Today was a Rat day, and the rat felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A firefly named Phleu flew down. "Little Rat," she said, "only you can bring the bell's voice back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings. Phleu smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the golden bell, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the rat, and it set off on its adventure, with Phleu right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Rat are quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Rats are natural problem-solvers who notice opportunities others miss.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was gold, the rat's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The rat tied a gold ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Phleu, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the rat's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The rat stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Rat can be restless or overly cautious with money and trust.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Phleu came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the rat climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Phleu. For the Rat today, that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the rat rang the golden bells 3 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The rat climbed 39 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a small golden crystal, humming a soft song. It was the heart of the bell!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the crystal, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Monday and Tuesday shone brightest, the Rat's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the crystal carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Phleu right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The rat placed the crystal inside the bell. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, dong! A deep, sweet sound rang across the land!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The bell sang again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The rat felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Rat, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 3, 39, and 232.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Phleu fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

សត្វអំពិលអំពែកមួយ ឈ្មោះភ្លឺ ហោះចុះមក។ ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "កណ្ដុរតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំសំឡេងកណ្ដឹងត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ឆ្នាំជូត ឆ្លាតវៃ និងរហ័សរហួន ហើយជាសត្វទីមួយនៃរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ។ ឆ្នាំជូតតំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា ភាពប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។ ភ្លឺញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីដាស់កណ្ដឹងមាស វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
កណ្ដុរនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជាមនុស្សឆ្លាតវៃ ប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងចូលចិត្តសេពគប់។ ជូតចេះរកឱកាសដែលអ្នកដទៃមើលមិនឃើញ។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌មាស ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់កណ្ដុរថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
កណ្ដុរចងខ្សែបូពណ៌មាស ជុំវិញក។ ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។"

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសនិរតី ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់កណ្ដុរ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែផ្លូវនោះ ឡើងភ្នំថ្មដ៏ចោត ហើយមានខ្យល់បក់ខ្លាំង។ កណ្ដុរឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជួនកាលមិនស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ឬប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេកចំពោះប្រាក់កាស និងទំនុកចិត្ត។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ភ្លឺមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ កណ្ដុរឡើងភ្នំ។ ខ្យល់ចាប់ផ្ដើមបក់ស្រាលវិញ ហើយនៅលើកំពូល វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់កណ្ដុរថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង កណ្ដុរវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនបី ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ កណ្ដុរឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនសាមសិបប្រាំបួន កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រីស្តាល់មាសតូចមួយ កំពុងបន្លឺបទចម្រៀងថ្នមៗ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃកណ្ដឹង!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគ្រីស្តាល់ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃអង្គារ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំជូត។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រីស្តាល់យ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ កណ្ដុរមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

```
វាគឺពណ៌មាស ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់កណ្ដុរថ្ងៃនេះ។

កណ្ដុរចងខ្សែបូពណ៌មាស ជុំវិញក។

ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

39
00:01:36,442 --> 00:01:38,796
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

40
00:01:38,846 --> 00:01:39,950
"

41

បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

42
00:01:42,797 --> 00:01:44,357
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

43

វាចង្អុលទៅទិសនិរតី ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់កណ្ដុរ។

45

ប៉ុន្តែផ្លូវនោះ ឡើងភ្នំថ្មដ៏ចោត

46
00:01:52,053 --> 00:01:53,394
ហើយមានខ្យល់បក់ខ្លាំង។

47

កណ្ដុរឈប់។

48

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជួនកាលមិនស្ងប់ស្ងាត់

49

ឬប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេកចំពោះប្រាក់កាស

50
00:01:59,073 --> 00:01:59,950
និងទំនុកចិត្ត។

51

ភ្លឺមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

52

ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

53
00:02:04,314 --> 00:02:06,028
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

54
00:02:06,078 --> 00:02:08,774
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

55
00:02:08,824 --> 00:02:09,950
"

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,365
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ កណ្ដុរឡើងភ្នំ។

57

58

ហើយនៅលើកំពូល វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

59

វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

60

តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

61

តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

62

ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

63

នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64

65

ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

66

ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

67

បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,275 --> 00:02:45,904
" សម្រាប់កណ្ដុរថ្ងៃនេះ

69
00:02:45,954 --> 00:02:48,194
គឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

70

ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

71

72

ចំនួន 3 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

73
00:02:56,771 --> 00:02:57,971

74
00:02:58,021 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

75
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,196
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

76
00:03:03,246 --> 00:03:06,003
កណ្ដុរឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 39 កាំ

77
00:03:06,053 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

78
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,652
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

79
00:03:11,702 --> 00:03:15,056
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រីស្តាល់មាសតូចមួយ

80
00:03:15,106 --> 00:03:17,610
កំពុងបន្លឺបទចម្រៀងថ្នមៗ។

81
00:03:17,660 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃកណ្ដឹង!

82

នៅជុំវិញគ្រីស្តាល់

83
00:03:21,224 --> 00:03:23,623
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

84
00:03:23,673 --> 00:03:25,868
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

85
00:03:25,918 --> 00:03:28,113
ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃអង្គារ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

86
00:03:28,163 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំជូត។

87
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,585
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

88
00:03:31,635 --> 00:03:35,623
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រីស្តាល់យ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

89
00:03:35,673 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

90
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

91
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

92
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

93
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,132
កណ្ដុរដាក់គ្រីស្តាល់ចូលក្នុងកណ្ដឹង។

94
00:03:53,182 --> 00:03:55,677
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

95
00:03:55,727 --> 00:03:56,950
បន្ទាប់មក ដុង!

96
00:03:57,000 --> 00:03:59,950
សំឡេងដ៏ពីរោះ បន្លឺឡើងពាសពេញទឹកដី!

97
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,677
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។

98
00:04:01,727 --> 00:04:02,950
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

99
00:04:03,000 --> 00:04:06,495
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

100
00:04:06,545 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

101
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,070
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

102
00:04:13,120 --> 00:04:14,910
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

103
00:04:14,960 --> 00:04:17,870
កណ្ដុរមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

104
00:04:17,920 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

105
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

106
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

107
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

108
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

109
00:04:31,654 --> 00:04:32,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

110
00:04:32,992 --> 00:04:35,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

111
00:04:35,827 --> 00:04:37,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

112
00:04:37,323 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 3 លេខ 39 និងលេខ 232។

113
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

114
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងភ្លឺ ក៏លង់លក់

115
00:04:47,528 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

116
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

117
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

118
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

119
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

120
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-29-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,713
Can one little golden rat bring the bell's

2
00:00:02,763 --> 00:00:05,213
voice back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,263 --> 00:00:07,976
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:08,026 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,181
But this morning

6
00:00:11,231 --> 00:00:13,335
the bell had lost its voice.

7
00:00:13,385 --> 00:00:14,719
When it was struck

8
00:00:14,769 --> 00:00:16,488
there was only silence.

9
00:00:16,538 --> 00:00:17,719
Without its song

10
00:00:17,769 --> 00:00:19,950
the festival could not begin.

11
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,965
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

12
00:00:23,015 --> 00:00:25,979
opened, and out came a little golden rat.

13
00:00:26,029 --> 00:00:27,376
Today was a Rat day

14
00:00:27,426 --> 00:00:29,950
and the rat felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,595
A firefly named Phleu flew down.

16
00:00:32,645 --> 00:00:34,413
"Little Rat," she said

17
00:00:34,463 --> 00:00:37,884
"only you can bring the bell's voice back.

18
00:00:37,934 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,036
Clever and quick, the first animal

20
00:00:42,086 --> 00:00:44,306
of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit

21
00:00:44,356 --> 00:00:46,514
resourcefulness, and new beginnings.

22
00:00:46,564 --> 00:00:47,312
Phleu smiled.

23
00:00:47,362 --> 00:00:48,539
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,589 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,819
To wake the golden bell, the little

26
00:00:52,869 --> 00:00:55,606
hero needed three lucky treasures:

27
00:00:55,656 --> 00:00:58,147
its lucky color, its lucky path

28
00:00:58,197 --> 00:00:59,950
and its lucky numbers.

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,807
"Let's go!" said the rat

30
00:01:02,857 --> 00:01:06,498
and it set off on its adventure

31
00:01:06,548 --> 00:01:09,950
with Phleu right by its side.

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,535
People born in the Year of the Rat are

33
00:01:12,585 --> 00:01:15,256
quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable.

34
00:01:15,306 --> 00:01:17,705
Rats are natural problem-solvers who

35
00:01:17,755 --> 00:01:19,950
notice opportunities others miss.

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,240
The first treasure was waiting

37
00:01:22,290 --> 00:01:24,912
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

38
00:01:24,962 --> 00:01:26,820
Only one shone brightest.

39
00:01:26,870 --> 00:01:29,950
It was gold, the rat's lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,646
The rat tied a gold

41
00:01:31,696 --> 00:01:33,700
ribbon around its neck.

42
00:01:33,750 --> 00:01:36,825
"Wear your lucky color," said Phleu

43
00:01:36,875 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

44
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,470
Next, they came to a crossroads

45
00:01:42,520 --> 00:01:43,771
with many paths.

46
00:01:43,821 --> 00:01:45,641
One path began to glow.

47
00:01:45,691 --> 00:01:47,836
It pointed to the southwest

48
00:01:47,886 --> 00:01:49,950
the rat's lucky direction.

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,093
But the path climbed a steep stone hill

50
00:01:52,143 --> 00:01:53,576
with a strong wind blowing.

51
00:01:53,626 --> 00:01:54,455
The rat stopped.

52
00:01:54,505 --> 00:01:55,664
Sometimes, people born

53
00:01:55,714 --> 00:01:57,752
in the Year of the Rat can be restless

54
00:01:57,802 --> 00:01:59,950
or overly cautious with money and trust.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,592
Phleu came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,642 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," she said.

57
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,055
Step by step, the rat climbed the hill.

60
00:02:14,105 --> 00:02:16,161
The wind grew softer

61
00:02:16,211 --> 00:02:19,950
and at the top, it laughed with joy.

62
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

63
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

64
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

65
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

66
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

67
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

68
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

69
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,525
"The door opens only

70
00:02:41,575 --> 00:02:43,966
at your best time," said Phleu.

71
00:02:44,016 --> 00:02:45,304
For the Rat today

72
00:02:45,354 --> 00:02:48,218
that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

73
00:02:48,268 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

74
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,529
When the time came

75
00:02:51,579 --> 00:02:54,775
the rat rang the golden bells 3 times

76
00:02:54,825 --> 00:02:56,792
its first lucky number.

77
00:02:56,842 --> 00:02:57,845
Ding!

78
00:02:57,895 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

79
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,104
The door opened

80
00:03:01,154 --> 00:03:03,642
and a stairway of light appeared.

81
00:03:03,692 --> 00:03:06,104
The rat climbed 39 glowing steps

82
00:03:06,154 --> 00:03:07,873
its second lucky number

83
00:03:07,923 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

84
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,140
At the top of the tower

85
00:03:12,190 --> 00:03:15,283
they found a small golden crystal

86
00:03:15,333 --> 00:03:17,188
humming a soft song.

87
00:03:17,238 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the bell!

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,882
Around the crystal, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,932 --> 00:03:25,815
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

90
00:03:25,865 --> 00:03:28,371
Monday and Tuesday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,421 --> 00:03:29,950
the Rat's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,950
Time was running out.

93
00:03:32,000 --> 00:03:34,712
Holding the crystal carefully

94
00:03:34,762 --> 00:03:37,379
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,429 --> 00:03:39,950
with Phleu right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,013
The rat placed the crystal

101
00:03:52,063 --> 00:03:53,283
inside the bell.

102
00:03:53,333 --> 00:03:55,744
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:55,794 --> 00:03:56,696
Then, dong!

104
00:03:56,746 --> 00:03:59,950
A deep, sweet sound rang across the land!

105
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,589
The bell sang again.

106
00:04:01,639 --> 00:04:03,720
Lanterns rose into the sky

107
00:04:03,770 --> 00:04:06,425
and the full-moon festival began.

108
00:04:06,475 --> 00:04:07,737
Everyone cheered

109
00:04:07,787 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

110
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,718
The full moon shone down warmly

111
00:04:12,768 --> 00:04:14,771
as if to say thank you.

112
00:04:14,821 --> 00:04:17,629
The rat felt peaceful and proud.

113
00:04:17,679 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

114
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,200
If you were born in the Year of the Rat

115
00:04:23,250 --> 00:04:25,033
today is your day too.

116
00:04:25,083 --> 00:04:27,700
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

117
00:04:27,750 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

118
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,908
Let's remember today's luck.

119
00:04:31,958 --> 00:04:33,167
Lucky color: gold.

120
00:04:33,217 --> 00:04:35,964
Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

121
00:04:36,014 --> 00:04:37,852
Lucky direction: southwest.

122
00:04:37,902 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 3, 39, and 232.

123
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,188
And so, under the bright full moon

124
00:04:43,238 --> 00:04:46,712
the little golden hero and Phleu fell

125
00:04:46,762 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

126
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

127
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

128
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

129
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

130
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

131
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Horse (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Horse, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Horse clashes with the Rat day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Horse as ingredients (the Horse is the only animal in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the horse hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Horse? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

2. Warning
   English: Year of the Horse, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមី ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Horse, here's why.

   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Horse? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Horse.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Horse? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 29 October, is a Rat day, opposite the Horse in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be impatient or struggle to commit long-term. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Rat day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Rat, Dragon and Monkey are lucky today, and the Horse should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Thursday, 29 October: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rat!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Rat are often described as quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
For love today, be warm and kind. A kind word or a small gift brings you closer.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time, and finish what matters most first.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
For money, keep it simple. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear it, or carry something gold with you.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើម សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ ថ្ងៃទីម្ភៃប្រាំបួន ខែតុលា ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ឆ្នាំជូត ឆ្លាតវៃ និងរហ័សរហួន ហើយជាសត្វទីមួយនៃរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ។ ឆ្នាំជូតតំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា ភាពប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា ឆ្លាតវៃ ប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងចូលចិត្តសេពគប់។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ សូមចាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ គិតមុននឹងចាយ ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌មាស។ សូមស្លៀក ឬយករបស់ពណ៌មាស ជាប់ខ្លួន។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសនិរតី។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)

ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ស ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)

Scene 36 (5:50–6:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-29-yt8.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,161
One sign has a Great Day today, two have

2
00:00:04,211 --> 00:00:08,055
a Good Day, and one should slow down.

3
00:00:08,105 --> 00:00:09,950
Which one are you?

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,795
Today is a Rat day.

5
00:00:11,845 --> 00:00:14,610
Coming up: one Great Day sign

6
00:00:14,660 --> 00:00:16,843
two Good Days, one sign

7
00:00:16,893 --> 00:00:19,950
to take it easy, and all twelve.

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,535
Rat, Dragon and Monkey are lucky today

9
00:00:23,585 --> 00:00:26,459
and the Horse should slow down.

10
00:00:26,509 --> 00:00:29,950
Find your sign in the chapters below.

11
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,674
Before we start

12
00:00:31,724 --> 00:00:36,042
comment your sign below, and subscribe

13
00:00:36,092 --> 00:00:39,950
so you get your fortune every day.

14
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:44,409
Thursday, 29 October: a Great Day

15
00:00:44,459 --> 00:00:49,950
for everyone born in the Year of the Rat!

16
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,128
Clever and quick, the first animal

17
00:00:53,178 --> 00:00:56,586
of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit

18
00:00:56,636 --> 00:00:59,950
resourcefulness, and new beginnings.

19
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,529
People born in the Year of the Rat

20
00:01:03,579 --> 00:01:07,213
are often described as quick-witted

21
00:01:07,263 --> 00:01:09,950
resourceful, and sociable.

22
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,464
Your luck is strong today.

23
00:01:13,514 --> 00:01:16,572
Say yes to good chances

24
00:01:16,622 --> 00:01:19,950
and share your happiness.

25
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:24,181
For love today, be warm and kind.

26
00:01:24,231 --> 00:01:27,642
A kind word or a small gift

27
00:01:27,692 --> 00:01:29,950
brings you closer.

28
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,145
For work and study

29
00:01:32,195 --> 00:01:35,682
take one clear step at a time

30
00:01:35,732 --> 00:01:39,950
and finish what matters most first.

31
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,283
For money, keep it simple.

32
00:01:43,333 --> 00:01:45,976
Plan before you spend

33
00:01:46,026 --> 00:01:49,950
and save a little for tomorrow.

34
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:54,197
Your lucky color today is gold.

35
00:01:54,247 --> 00:01:59,950
Wear it, or carry something gold with you.

36
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,904
Your best time is

37
00:02:01,954 --> 00:02:05,122
from 3 to 5 in the afternoon

38
00:02:05,172 --> 00:02:09,950
and your lucky direction is the southwest.

39
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,437
Your lucky numbers: 3, 39, and 232.

40
00:02:14,487 --> 00:02:17,386
Comment your sign below

41
00:02:17,436 --> 00:02:19,950
and save this video!

42
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,628
The Rat's lucky days of the week

43
00:02:23,678 --> 00:02:26,272
are Monday and Tuesday.

44
00:02:26,322 --> 00:02:29,950
Keep them for plans that matter.

45
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,681
A gentle reminder:

46
00:02:31,731 --> 00:02:34,181
Rat people can be restless

47
00:02:34,231 --> 00:02:38,027
or overly cautious with money and trust.

48
00:02:38,077 --> 00:02:39,950
Stay balanced today.

49
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:44,609
Next, the Year of the Dragon: a Good Day!

50
00:02:44,659 --> 00:02:46,541
The Dragon shares

51
00:02:46,591 --> 00:02:49,950
a lucky triangle with the Rat.

52
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:55,664
Team up with a friend today.

53
00:02:55,714 --> 00:02:59,950
Shared plans go well.

54
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:05,082
Your lucky color is gray, and your best

55
00:03:05,132 --> 00:03:09,950
time is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

56
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:15,538
Your lucky direction is the southwest.

57
00:03:15,588 --> 00:03:19,950
Lucky numbers: 7, 16, and 711.

58
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:23,045
Dragon friends, stay close

59
00:03:23,095 --> 00:03:25,902
to the Rat people today.

60
00:03:25,952 --> 00:03:29,950
Good luck grows when you share it.

61
```

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes a new voice for every clip, so even with the same words the voice can drift a little between scenes. Two ways to keep Chanda's voice the same:
1. Most reliable – one voice-over for the whole video: make every scene with "Copy, no voice" (music only; in Chanda's on-camera scenes her lips still move). Then record the whole "Voice-over" text of that video in one go, yourself or with one text-to-speech voice (for Khmer, for example Azure's km-KH-SreymomNeural; for English, choose one voice and always use it), and lay it over the clips in CapCut, using the subtitle file to line it up. One recording = one voice from start to end.
2. Quicker – Flow's own voice: always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice" (never retype or shorten the voice text: it describes the exact same voice every time), make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, and listen to each clip; if one sounds different, make only that clip again.
Do not mix the two ways in one video.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. Setting: a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a glowing full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, floating lotus flowers, ornate pink-and-gold lotus details, magical sparkles and warm golden reflections. Cinematic lighting, rich colors, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, gentle slow camera movement, professional animated-movie rendering. The zodiac animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos.

## Reference pictures (make these first, so each animal looks the same in every scene)
Optional ready-made references: your site's golden animal pictures, e.g. https://mybirthsign.com/images/profile/spirit-tiger.webp. Make each picture once, then add it as a reference image ("ingredient") in Flow, or use it as the starting image of a scene.

## Scene 1 – Rat day (Chanda on camera)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda matches her reference image. Then: magical and powerful. An adorable golden rat with big sparkling eyes, tiny jade-green ear jewels and a proud little smile stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it leaps off the lotus and dashes through the air in a spiral, leaving a trail of glittering gold coins and stars. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. An adorable golden rat with big sparkling eyes, tiny jade-green ear jewels and a proud little smile, an adorable but mighty golden dragon with big friendly eyes, shimmering scales, flowing whiskers and a glowing pearl and a playful, adorable golden monkey with big curious eyes and a cheeky smile fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Horse, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A proud, adorable golden horse with big sparkling eyes and a flowing shining mane curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The horse matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

Two files are saved next to this one: `2026-10-29.srt` (Khmer) and `2026-10-29.en.srt` (English).
Upload in YouTube Studio → Subtitles → Add language → choose Khmer or English → Upload file → "With timing". You can add both to the same video.
The timings are estimates for a 45-second voice-over; after you record the voice, drag each line to match in YouTube's subtitle editor, or use "Auto-sync".
